# The slot completion routines (SlotCompletionPercent, SlotClearedFlags, SlotLastClearedFlag, DrawSlotPercent, CountSetBits):
# the arms that count a set bit or mark a flag are in no trace, so the draft cannot run them; these cases keep every counted byte
# and mask zero so the routines are compared on the arms that were traced (run with --cases 0 or beside the seeded ones).
